computeForces(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    \n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n        \n        for (size_t j = 0; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;\n            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;\n            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;\n            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n            \n            Fx += dx * invDist3;\n            Fy += dy * invDist3;\n            Fz += dz * invDist3;\n        }\n        \n        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;\n        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;\n        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;\n        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;\n        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;\n    }\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    \n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x + \n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y + \n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n    \n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        for (size_t j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n            const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;\n            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;\n            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            energy -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n        \n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n        if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n            return false;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"N-Body Simulation\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of bodies: %d\\n\", numBodies);\n    printf(\"Number of steps: %d\\n\", numSteps);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    \n    // Initialize bodies\n    std::vector<Body> bodies(numBodies);\n    randomizeBodies(bodies);\n    \n    // Run simulation\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    \n    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {\n        computeForces(bodies);\n        integrateBodies(bodies);\n    }\n    \n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n    \n    printf(\"Simulation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n    \n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing\n        std::vector<double> bodyData;\n        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);\n        for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);\n        }\n        print_results(bodyData, \"Bodies\");\n    }\n    \n    // Validation: check that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\n    if (validate) {\n        printf(\"Validating simulation results...\\n\");\n        \n        if (validateSimulation(bodies)) {\n            // Report final energy for reference\n            double finalEnergy = computeTotalEnergy(bodies);\n            printf(\"Final energy: %.6f\\n\", finalEnergy);\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Validation: FAILED\\n\");\n k < BLOCK_SIZE; ++k) {\n            const double dx = sx[k] - xi;\n            const double dy = sy[k] - yi;\n            const double dz = sz[k] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            Fx += dx * invDist3;\n            Fy += dy * invDist3;\n            Fz += dz * invDist3;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    // Remainder tile\n    const int tailStart = fullTiles * BLOCK_SIZE;\n    const int tail = n - tailStart;\n    if (tail > 0) {\n        const int j = tailStart + threadIdx.x;\n        if (threadIdx.x < tail) {\n            sx[threadIdx.x] = px[j];\n            sy[threadIdx.x] = py[j];\n            sz[threadIdx.x] = pz[j];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        for (int k = 0; k < tail; ++k) {\n            const double dx = sx[k] - xi;\n            const double dy = sy[k] - yi;\n            const double dz = sz[k] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            Fx += dx * invDist3;\n            Fy += dy * invDist3;\n            Fz += dz * invDist3;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (active) {\n        vx[local] += DT * Fx;\n        vy[local] += DT * Fy;\n        vz[local] += DT * Fz;\n    }\n}\n\n__global__ void integrateKernel(double* __restrict__ px, double* __restrict__ py, double* __restrict__ pz,\n                                const double* __restrict__ vx, const double* __restrict__ vy,\n                                const double* __restrict__ vz, const int start, const int count) {\n    const int local = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (local >= count) return;\n    const int i = start + local;\n    px[i] += vx[local] * DT;\n    py[i] += vy[local] * DT;\n    pz[i] += vz[local] * DT;\n}\n\n// Potential-energy partial sums: partial[i] = sum_{j>i} 1/dist(i,j). Bodies are distributed\n// cyclically across ranks because the work per i shrinks with i.\n__global__ void potentialKernel(const double* __restrict__ px, const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                const double* __restrict__ pz, double* __restrict__ partial, const int n,\n                                const int rank, const int numRanks) {\n    const int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int i = rank + idx * numRanks;\n    if (i >= n) return;\n\n    const double xi = px[i], yi = py[i], zi = pz[i];\n    double sum = 0.0;\n    for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n        const double dx = px[j] - xi;\n        const double dy = py[j] - yi;\n        const double dz = pz[j] - zi;\n        const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n        sum -= 1.0 / dist;\n    }\n    partial[i] = sum;\n}\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Kinetic energy on the host (O(n), kept in the original order), potential energy from the\n// distributed GPU partials.\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<double>& potentialPartials) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)\n    for (const double partial : potentialPartials) {\n        energy += partial;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    // 0 = ok, 1 = non-finite, 2 = position out of bounds, 3 = velocity out of bounds\n    std::vector<unsigned char> status(n, 0);\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const Body& body = bodies[i];\n\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            status[i] = 1;\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            status[i] = 2;\n        } else if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            status[i] = 3;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Report the first offending body, matching the sequential order of the original check\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        switch (status[i]) {\n            case 0: continue;\n            case 1: printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\"); return false;\n            case 2: printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\"); return false;\n            default: printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\"); return false;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Pick the GPU for this rank based on its position within the node.\nstatic void selectDevice(MPI_Comm comm, int rank) {\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(comm, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int nodeRank = 0;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &nodeRank);\n    MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));\n    if (deviceCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA devices available\\n\");\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(nodeRank % deviceCount));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));  // force context creation outside the timed region\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided =      numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = The inner loop walks j in ascending order,\n// exactly like the sequential reference, so the summation order (and thus the result) is preserved.\n__global__ void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ px, const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                    const double* __restrict__ pz, double* __restrict__ vx, double* __restrict__ vy,\n                                    double* __restrict__ vz, const int n, const int start, const int count) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK_SIZE], sy[BLOCK_SIZE], sz[BLOCK_SIZE];\n\n    const int local = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = local < count;\n    const int i = start + local;\n\n    const double xi = active ? px[i] : 0.0;\n    const double yi = active ? py[i] : 0.0;\n    const double zi = active ? pz[i] : 0.0;\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n    const int fullTiles = n / BLOCK_SIZE;\n    for (int tile = 0; tile < fullTiles; ++tile) {\n        const int j = tile * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n        sx[threadIdx.x] = px[j];\n        sy[threadIdx.x] = py[j];\n        sz[threadIdx.x] = pz[j];\n        __syncthreads();\n\n#pragma unroll 8\n        for (int k = 0; k < BLOCK_SIZE; ++k) {\n            const double dx = sx[k] - xi;\n            const double dy = sy[k] - yi;\n            const double dz = sz[k] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            Fx += dx * invDist3;\n            Fy += dy * invDist3;\n            Fz += dz * invDist3;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    // Remainder tile\n    const int tailStart = fullTiles * BLOCK_SIZE;\n    const int tail = n - tailStart;\n    if (tail > 0) {\n        const int j = tailStart + threadIdx.x;\n        if (threadIdx.x < tail) {\n            sx[threadIdx.x] = px[j];\n            sy[threadIdx.x] = py[j];\n            sz[threadIdx.x] = pz[j];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        for (int k = 0; k < tail; ++k) {\n            const double dx = sx[k] - xi;\n            const double dy = sy[k] - yi;\n            const double dz = sz[k] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            Fx += dx * invDist3;\n            Fy += dy * invDist3;\n            Fz += dz * invDist3;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (active) {\n        vx[local] += DT * Fx;\n        vy[local] += DT * Fy;\n        vz[local] += DT * Fz;\n    }\n}\n\n__global__ void integrateKernel(double* __restrict__ px, double* __restrict__ py, double* __restrict__ pz,\n                                const double* __restrict__ vx, const double* __restrict__ vy,\n                                const double* __restrict__ vz, const int start, const int count) {\n    const int local = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (local >= count) return;\n    const int i = start + local;\n    px[i] += vx[local] * DT;\n    py[i] += vy[local] * DT;\n    pz[i] += vz[local] * DT;\n}\n\n// Potential-energy partial sums: partial[i] = sum_{j>i} 1/dist(i,j). Bodies are distributed\n// cyclically across ranks because the work per i shrinks with i.\n__global__ void potentialKernel(const double* __restrict__ px, const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                const double* __restrict__ pz, double* __restrict__ partial, const int n,\n                                const int rank, const int numRanks) {\n    const int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int i = rank + idx * numRanks;\n    if (i >= n) return;\n\n    const double xi = px[i], yi = py[i], zi = pz[i];\n    double sum = 0.0;\n    for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n        const double dx = px[j] - xi;\n        const double dy = py[j] - yi;\n        const double dz = pz[j] - zi;\n        const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n        sum -= 1.0 / dist;\n    }\n    partial[i] = sum;\n}\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Kinetic energy on the host (O(n), kept in the original order), potential energy from the\n// distributed GPU partials.\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<double>& potentialPartials) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)\n    for (const double partial : potentialPartials) {\n        energy += partial;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    // 0 = ok, 1 = non-finite, 2 = position out of bounds, 3 = velocity out of bounds\n    std::vector<unsigned char> status(n, 0);\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const Body& body = bodies[i];\n\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            status[i] = 1;\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            status[i] = 2;\n        } else if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            status[i] = 3;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Report the first offending body, matching the sequential order of the original check\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        switch (status[i]) {\n            case 0: continue;\n            case 1: printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\"); return false;\n            case 2: printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\"); return false;\n            default: printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\"); return false;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Pick the GPU for this rank based on its position within the node.\nstatic void selectDevice(MPI_Comm comm, int rank) {\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(comm, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int nodeRank = 0;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &nodeRank);\n    MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));\n    if (deviceCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA devices available\\n\");\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(nodeRank % deviceCount));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));  // force context creation outside the timed region\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n\n    int rank = 0, numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n    const bool isRoot = (rank == 0);\n\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i      numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / std::sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n            \n            Fx += dx * invDist3;\n            Fy += dy * invDist3;\n            Fz += dz * invDist3;\n        }\n        \n        bodies[i].vel.x += DT * Fx;\n        bodies[i].vel.y += DT * Fy;\n        bodies[i].vel.z += DT * Fz;\n    }\n}\n\nvoid integrateBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x += body.vel.x * DT;\n        body.pos.y += body.vel.y * DT;\n        body.pos.z += body.vel.z * DT;\n    }\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    \n    // const double dx = bodies[j].pos.x - bodies[i].pos.x;\n            const double dy = bodies[j].pos.y - bodies[i].pos.y;\n            const double dz = bodies[j].pos.z - bodies[i].pos.z;\n            const double dist = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            energy -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n Bodies are distributed\n// cyclically across ranks because the work per i shrinks with i.\n__global__ void potentialKernel(const double* __restrict__ px, const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                const double* __restrict__ pz, double* __restrict__ partial, const int n,\n                                const int rank, const int numRanks) {\n    const int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int i = rank + idx * numRanks;\n    if (i >= n) return;\n\n    const double xi = px[i], yi = py[i], zi = pz[i];\n    double sum = 0.0;\n    for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n        const double dx = px[j] - xi;\n        const double dy = py[j] - yi;\n        const double dz = pz[j] - zi;\n        const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Kinetic energy on the host (O(n), kept in the original order), potential energy from the\n// distributed GPU partials.\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<double>& potentialPartials) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)\n    for (const double partial : potentialPartials) {\n        energy += partial;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable std::vector<unsigned char> status(n, 0);\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const Body& body = bodies[i];\n\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            status[i] = 1;\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            status[i] = 2;\n        } else if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            status[i] = 3;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Report the first offending body, matching the sequential order of the original check\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        switch (status[i]) {\n            case 0: continue;\n            case 1: printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\"); return false;\n            case 2: printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\"); return false;\n            default: printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\"); return false;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Pick the GPU for this rank based on its position within the node.\nstatic void selectDevice(MPI_Comm comm, int rank) {\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(comm, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int nodeRank = 0;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &nodeRank);\n    MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n\n    int rank = 0, numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n    const bool isRoot = (rank == 0);\n\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (isRoot) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (isRoot) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    selectDevice(MPI_COMM_WORLD, rank);\n\n    if (isRoot) {\n        printf(\"N-Body Simulation\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of bodies: %d\\n\", numBodies);\n        printf(\"Number of steps: %d\\n\", numSteps);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    const int n = numBodies;\n    if (n <= 0) {\n        if (isRoot) {\n            printf(\"Simulation time: 0 ms\\n\");\n            if (printResults) print_results(std::vector<double>{}, \"Bodies\");\n            if (validate) {\n                printf(\"Validating simulation results...\\n\");\n                printf(\"Final energy: %.6f\\n\", 0.0);\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            }\n        }\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return 0;\n numRanks ? 1 : 0);\n        displs[r] = (r == 0) ? 0 : displs[r - 1] + counts[r - 1];\n    }\n    const int start = displs[rank];\n    const int count = counts[rank];\n\n    // Initialize bodies (identical on every rank; the RNG stream is inherently sequential)\n    std::vector<Body> bodies(n);\n    randomizeBodies(bodies);\n\n    // Pinned host staging buffers for the per-step position                                                                      \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                            \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d\\n\", cudaGetErrorString(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__);        \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                                     \\\n        }                                                                                                    \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// Structure-of-arrays state. Positions are replicated on every rank (needed by every interaction),\n// velocities only exist for the bodies a rank owns.\nstruct DeviceState {\n    double *px = nullptr, *py = nullptr, *pz = nullptr;  // n entries (all bodies)\n    double *vx = nullptr, *vy = nullptr, *vz = nullptr;  // count entries (owned bodies)\n    double* partial = nullptr;                           // n entries, potential-energy partials\n};\n\n// Force accumulation for the locally owned bodies. The inner loop walks j in ascending order,\n// exactly like the sequential reference, so the summation order (and thus the result) is preserved.\n__global__ void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ px, const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                    const double* __restrict__ pz, double* __restrict__ vx, double* __restrict__ vy,\n                                    double* __restrict__ vz, const int n, const int start, const int count) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK_SIZE], sy[BLOCK_SIZE], sz[BLOCK_SIZE];\n\n    const int local = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = local < count;\n    const int i = start + local;\n\n    const double xi = active ? px[i] : 0.0;\n    const double yi = active ? py[i] : 0.0;\n    const double zi = active ? pz[i] : 0.0;\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n    const int fullTiles = n / BLOCK_SIZE;\n    for (int tile = 0; tile < fullTiles; ++tile) {\n        const int j = tile * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n        sx[threadIdx.x] = px[j];\n        sy[threadIdx.x] = py[j];\n        sz[threadIdx.x] = pz[j];\n        __syncthreads();\n\n#pragma unroll 8\n        for (int k = 0; k < BLOCK_SIZE; ++k) {\n            const double dx = sx[k] - xi;\n            const double dy = sy[k] - yi;\n            const double dz = sz[k] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            Fx += dx * invDist3;\n            Fy += dy * invDist3;\n            Fz += dz * invDist3;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    // Remainder tile\n    const int tailStart = fullTiles * BLOCK_SIZE;\n    const int tail = n - tailStart;\n    if (tail > 0) {\n        const int j = tailStart + threadIdx.x;\n        if (threadIdx.x < tail) {\n            sx[threadIdx.x] = px[j];\n            sy[threadIdx.x] = py[j];\n            sz[threadIdx.x] = pz[j];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        for (int k = 0; k < tail; ++k) {\n            const double dx = sx[k] - xi;\n            const double dy = sy[k] - yi;\n            const double dz = sz[k] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            Fx += dx * invDist3;\n            Fy += dy * invDist3;\n            Fz += dz * invDist3;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (active) {\n        vx[local] += DT * Fx;\n        vy[local] += DT * Fy;\n        vz[local] += DT * Fz;\n    }\n}\n\n__global__ void integrateKernel(double* __restrict__ px, double* __restrict__ py, double* __restrict__ pz,\n                                const double* __restrict__ vx, const double* __restrict__ vy,\n                                const double* __restrict__ vz, const int start, const int count) {\n    const int local = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (local >= count) return;\n    const int i = start + local;\n    px[i] += vx[local] * DT;\n    py[i] += vy[local] * DT;\n    pz[i] += vz[local] * DT;\n}\n\n// Potential-energy partial sums: partial[i] = sum_{j>i} 1/dist(i,j). Bodies are distributed\n// cyclically across ranks because the work per i shrinks with i.\n__global__ void potentialKernel(const double* __restrict__ px, const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                const double* __restrict__ pz, double* __restrict__ partial, const int n,\n                                const int rank, const int numRanks) {\n    const int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int i = rank + idx * numRanks;\n    if (i >= n) return;\n\n    const double xi = px[i], yi = py[i], zi = pz[i];\n    double sum = 0.0;\n    for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n        const double dx = px[j] - xi;\n        const double dy = py[j] - yi;\n        const double dz = pz[j] - zi;\n        const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n        sum -= 1.0 / dist;\n    }\n    partial[i] = sum;\n}\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Kinetic energy on the host (O(n), kept in the original order), potential energy from the\n// distributed GPU partials.\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<double>& potentialPartials) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)\n    for (const double partial : potentialPartials) {\n        energy += partial;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    // 0 = ok, 1 = non-finite, 2 = position out of bounds, 3 = velocity out of bounds\n    std::vector<unsigned char> status(n, 0);\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const Body& body = bodies[i];\n\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            status[i] = 1;\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            status[i] = 2;\n        } else if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            status[i] = 3;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Report the first offending body, matching the sequential order of the original check\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        switch (status[i]) {\n            case 0: continue;\n            case 1: printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\"); return false;\n            case 2: printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\"); return false;\n            default: printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\"); return false;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Kinetic energy on the host (O(n), kept in the original order), potential energy from the\n// distributed GPU partials.\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<double>& potentialPartials) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)\n    for (const double partial : potentialPartials) {\n        energy += partial;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    // 0 = ok, 1 = non-finite, 2 = position out of bounds, 3 = velocity out of bounds\n    std::vector<unsigned char> status(n, 0);\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const Body& body = bodies[i];\n\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            status[i] = 1;\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            status[i] = 2;\n        } else if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            status[i] = 3;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Report the first offending body, matching the sequential order of the original check\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        switch (status[i]) {\n            case 0: continue;\n            case 1: printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\"); return false;\n            case 2: printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\"); return false;\n            default: printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\"); return false;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", Positions are replicated on every rank (needed by every interaction),\n// velocities only exist for the bodies a rank owns.\nstruct DeviceState {\n    double *px = nullptr, *py = nullptr, *pz = nullptr;  // n entries (all bodies)\n    double *vx = nullptr, *vy = nullptr, *vz = nullptr;  // count entries (owned bodies)\n    double* partial = nullptr;                           // n entries, potential-energy partials\n};\n\n// Force accumulation for the locally owned bodies. The inner loop walks j in ascending order,\n// exactly like the sequential reference, so the summation order (and thus the result) is preserved.\n__global__ void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ px, const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                    const double* __restrict__ pz, double* __restrict__ vx, double* __restrict__ vy,\n                                    double* __restrict__ vz, const int n, const int start, const int count) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK_SIZE], sy[BLOCK_SIZE], sz[BLOCK_SIZE];\n\n    const int local = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = local < count;\n    const int i = start + local;\n\n    const double xi = active ? px[i] : 0.0;\n    const double yi = active ? py[i] : 0.0;\n    const double zi = active ? pz[i] : 0.0;\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n    const int fullTiles = n / BLOCK_SIZE;\n    for (int tile = 0; k < tail; ++k) {\n            const double dx = sx[k] - xi;\n            const double dy = sy[k] - yi;\n            const double dz = sz[k] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = distanceSquared(dx, dy, dz);\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            Fx = fma(dx, invDist3, Fx);\n            Fy = fma(dy, invDist3, Fy);\n            Fz = fma(dz, invDist3, Fz);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (active) {\n        vx[local] = fma(DT, Fx, vx[local]);\n        vy[local] = fma(DT, Fy, vy[local]);\n        vz[local] = fma(DT, Fz, vz[local]);\n    }\n}\n\n__global__ void integrateKernel(double* __restrict__ px, double* __restrict__ py, double* __restrict__ pz,\n                                const double* __restrict__ vx, const double* __restrict__ vy,\n                                const double* __restrict__ vz, const int start, const int count) {\n    const int local = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (local >= count) return;\n    const int i = start + local;\n    px[i] += vx[local] * DT;\n    py[i] += vy[local] * DT;\n    pz[i] += vz[local] * DT;\n}\n\n// Potential-energy partial sums: partial[i] = sum_{j>i} 1/dist(i,j). Bodies are distributed\n// cyclically across ranks because the work per i shrinks with i.\n__global__ void potentialKernel(const double* __restrict__ px, const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                const double* __restrict__ pz, double* __restrict__ partial, const int n,\n                                const int rank, const int numRanks) {\n    const int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int i = rank + idx * numRanks;\n    if (i >= n) return;\n\n    const double xi = px[i], yi = py[i], zi = pz[i];\n    double sum = 0.0;\n    for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n        const double dx = px[j] - xi;\n        const double dy = py[j] - yi;\n        const double dz = pz[j] - zi;\n        const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n        sum -= 1.0 / dist;\n    }\n    partial[i] = sum;\n}\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Kinetic energy on the host (O(n), kept in the original order), potential energy from the\n// distributed GPU partials.\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<double>& potentialPartials) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)\n    for (const double partial : potentialPartials) {\n        energy += partial;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    // 0 = ok, 1 = non-finite, 2 = position out of bounds, 3 = velocity out of bounds\n    std::vector<unsigned char> status(n, 0);\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const Body& body = bodies[i];\n\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            status[i] = 1;\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            status[i] = 2;\n        } else if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            status[i] = 3;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Report the first offending body, matching the sequential order of the original check\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        switch (status[i]) {\n            case 0: continue;\n            case 1: printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\"); return false;\n            case 2: printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\"); return false;\n            default: printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\"); return false;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Pick the GPU for this rank based on its position within the node.\nstatic void selectDevice(MPI_Comm comm, int rank) {\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(comm, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int nodeRank = 0;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &nodeRank);\n    MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));\n    if (deviceCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA devices available\\n\");\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(nodeRank % deviceCount));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));  // force context creation outside the timed region\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n\n    int rank = 0, numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n    const bool isRoot = (rank == 0);\n\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (isRoot) printUsage(argv[0]);\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (isRoot) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    selectDevice(MPI_COMM_WORLD, rank);\n\n    if (isRoot) {\n        printf(\"N-Body Simulation\\n\");\n        printf(\"Number of bodies: %d\\n\", numBodies);\n        printf(\"Number of steps: %d\\n\", numSteps);\n        printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    }\n\n    const int n = numBodies;\n    if (n <= 0) {\n        if (isRoot) {\n            printf(\"Simulation time: 0 ms\\n\");\n            if (printResults) print_results(std::vector<double>{}, \"Bodies\");\n            if (validate) {\n                printf(\"Validating simulation results...\\n\");\n                printf(\"Final energy: %.6f\\n\", 0.0);\n                printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n            }\n        }\n        MPI_Finalize();\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    // Block distribution of bodies over ranks\n    std::vector<int> counts(numRanks), displs(numRanks);\n    for (int r = 0; r < numRanks; ++r) {\n        counts[r] = n / numRanks + (r < n % numRanks ? 1 : 0);\n        displs[r] = (r == 0) ? 0 : displs[r - 1] + counts[r - 1];\n    }\n    const int start = displs[rank];\n    const int count = counts[rank];\n\n    // Initialize bodies (identical on every rank; the RNG stream is inherently sequential)\n    std::vector<Body> bodies(n);\n    randomizeBodies(bodies);\n\n    // Pinned host staging buffers for the per-step position exchange\n    double *hx = nullptr, *hy = nullptr, *hz = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&hx, n * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&hy, n * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&hz, n * sizeof(double)));\n\n    std::vector<double> vxHost(count), vyHost(count), vzHost(count);\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i)  vxHost[i] = bodies[start + i].vel.x;\n        vyHost[i] = bodies[start + i].vel.y;\n        vzHost[i] = bodies[start + i].vel.z;\n    }\n\n    DeviceState d;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d.px, n * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d.py, n * sizeof(double)));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d.pz, n * sizeof(double)));\n    const size_t velBytes = (count > 0 ? count : 1) * sizeof(double);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d.vx, velBytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d.vy, velBytes));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d.vz, velBytes));\n\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d.px, hx, n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d.py, hy, n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d.pz, hz, n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n    if (count > 0) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d.vx, vxHost.data(), count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d.vy,        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(stream));\n        }\n    }\n\n    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n    auto end_time = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end_time - start_time);\n\n    if (isRoot) printf(\"Simulation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Collect the final state on the root sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(vzHost.data(), d.vz, count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n    }\n\n    std::vector<double> allVx(isRoot ? n : 0), Positions are replicated on every rank (needed by every interaction),\n// velocities only exist for the bodies a rank owns.\nstruct DeviceState {\n    double *px = nullptr, *py = nullptr, *pz = nullptr;  // n entries (all bodies)\n    double *vx = nullptr, *vy = nullptr, *vz = nullptr;  // count entries (owned bodies)\n    double* partial = nullptr;                           // n entries, potential-energy partials\n};\n\n// The reference build lets the host compiler contract these expressions into FMAs; the device code\n// spells the same contractions out explicitly (and is compiled with -fmad=false) so that both\n// produce bit-identical results.\n__device__ __forceinline__ double distanceSquared(const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    return fma(dz, dz, fma(dy, dy, dx * dx)) + SOFTENING;\n}\n\n// Force accumulation for the locally owned bodies. The inner loop walks j in ascending order,\n// exactly like the sequential reference, so the summation order (and thus the result) is preserved.\n__global__ void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ px, const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                    const double* __restrict__ pz, double* __restrict__ vx, double* __restrict__ vy,\n                                    double* __restrict__ vz, const int n, const int start, const int count) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK_SIZE], sy[BLOCK_SIZE], sz[BLOCK_SIZE];\n\n    const int local = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = local < count;\n    const int i = start + local;\n\n    const double xi = active ? px[i] : 0.0;\n    const double yi = active ? py[i] : 0.0;\n    const double zi = active ? pz[i] : 0.0;\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n    const int fullTiles = n / BLOCK_SIZE;\n    for (int tile = 0; tile < fullTiles; ++tile) {\n        const int j = tile * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n        sx[threadIdx.x] = px[j];\n        sy[threadIdx.x] = py[j];\n        sz[threadIdx.x] = pz[j];\n        __syncthreads();\n\n#pragma unroll 8\n        for (int k = 0; k < BLOCK_SIZE; ++k) {\n            const double dx = sx[k] - xi;\n            const double dy = sy[k] - yi;\n            const double dz = sz[k] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = distanceSquared(dx, dy, dz);\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            Fx = fma(dx, invDist3, Fx);\n            Fy = fma(dy, invDist3, Fy);\n            Fz = fma(dz, invDist3, Fz);\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    // Remainder tile\n    const int tailStart = fullTiles * BLOCK_SIZE;\n    const int tail = n - tailStart;\n    if (tail > 0) {\n        const int j = tailStart + threadIdx.x;\n        if (threadIdx.x < tail) {\n            sx[threadIdx.x] = px[j];\n            sy[threadIdx.x] = py[j];\n            sz[threadIdx.x] = pz[j];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        for (int k = 0; k < tail; ++k) {\n            const double dx = sx[k] - xi;\n            const double dy = sy[k] - yi;\n            const double dz = sz[k] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = distanceSquared(dx, dy, dz);\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            Fx = fma(dx, invDist3, Fx);\n            Fy = fma(dy, invDist3, Fy);\n            Fz = fma(dz, invDist3, Fz);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (active) {\n        vx[local] = fma(DT, Fx, vx[local]);\n        vy[local] = fma(DT, Fy, vy[local]);\n        vz[local] = fma(DT, Fz, vz[local]);\n    }\n}\n\n__global__ void integrateKernel(double* __restrict__ px, double* __restrict__ py, double* __restrict__ pz,\n                                const double* __restrict__ vx, const double* __restrict__ vy,\n                                const double* __restrict__ vz, const int start, const int count) {\n    const int local = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (local >= count) return;\n    const int i = start + local;\n    px[i] += vx[local] * DT;\n    py[i] += vy[local] * DT;\n    pz[i] += vz[local] * DT;\n}\n\n// Potential-energy partial sums: partial[i] = sum_{j>i} 1/dist(i,j). Bodies are distributed\n// cyclically across ranks because the work per i shrinks with i.\n__global__ void potentialKernel(const double* __restrict__ px, const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                const double* __restrict__ pz, double* __restrict__ partial, const int n,\n                                const int rank, const int numRanks) {\n    const int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int i = rank + idx * numRanks;\n    if (i >= n) return;\n\n    const double xi = px[i], yi = py[i], zi = pz[i];\n    double sum = 0.0;\n    for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n        const double dx = px[j] - xi;\n        const double dy = py[j] - yi;\n        const double dz = pz[j] - zi;\n        const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n        sum -= 1.0 / dist;\n    }\n    partial[i] = sum;\n}\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Kinetic energy on the host (O(n), kept in the original order), potential energy from the\n// distributed GPU partials.\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const std::vector<double>& potentialPartials) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)\n    for (const double partial : potentialPartials) {\n        energy += partial;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    // 0 = ok, 1 = non-finite, 2 = position out of bounds, 3 = velocity out of bounds\n    std::vector<unsigned char> status(n, 0);\n\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        const Body& body = bodies[i];\n\n        // Check for NaN or Inf values\n        if (!std::isfinite(body.pos.x) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.y) || !std::isfinite(body.pos.z) ||\n            !std::isfinite(body.vel.x) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.y) || !std::isfinite(body.vel.z)) {\n            status[i] = 1;\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            status[i] = 2;\n        } else if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            status[i] = 3;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Report the first offending body, matching the sequential order of the original check\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        switch (status[i]) {\n            case 0: continue;\n            case 1: printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\"); return false;\n            case 2: printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\"); return false;\n            default: printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\"); return false;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Pick the GPU for this rank based on its position within the node.\nstatic void selectDevice(MPI_Comm comm, int rank) {\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(comm, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL, &nodeComm);\n    int nodeRank = 0;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(nodeComm, &nodeRank);\n    MPI_Comm_free(&nodeComm);\n\n    int deviceCount = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount));\n    if (deviceCount == 0) {\n        fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA devices available\\n\");\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(nodeRank % deviceCount));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));  // force context creation outside the timed region\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n\n    int rank = 0, numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n    const bool isRoot = (rank == 0);\n\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = The inner loop walks j in ascending order,\n55\t// exactly like the sequential reference, so the summation order (and thus the result) is preserved.\n56\t__global__ void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ px, const double* __restrict__ py,\n57\t                                    const double* __restrict__ pz, double* __restrict__ vx, double* __restrict__ vy,\n58\t                                    double* __restrict__ vz, const int n, const int start, const int count) {\n59\t    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK_SIZE], sy[BLOCK_SIZE], sz[BLOCK_SIZE];\n60\t\n61\t    const int local = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n62\t    const bool active = local < count;\n63\t    const int i = start + local;\n64\t\n65\t    const double xi = active ? px[i] : 0.0;\n66\t    const double yi = active ? py[i] : 0.0;\n67\t    const double zi = active ? pz[i] : 0.0;\n68\t\n69\t    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n70\t\n71\t    const int fullTiles = n / BLOCK_SIZE;\n72\t    for (int tile = 0; The inner loop walks j in ascending order,\n// exactly like the sequential reference, so the summation order (and thus the result) is preserved.\n// The block size doubles as the shared-memory tile size; it is chosen at launch time so that even a\n// small number of local bodies is spread over all SMs (see chooseBlockSize).\ntemplate <int BS>\n__global__ void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ px, const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                    const double* __restrict__ pz, double* __restrict__ vx, double* __restrict__ vy,\n                                    double* __restrict__ vz, const int n, const int start, const int count) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BS], sy[BS], sz[BS];\n\n    const int local = blockIdx.x * BS + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = local < count;\n    const int i = start + local;\n\n    const double xi = active ? px[i] : 0.0;\n    const double yi = active ? py[i] : 0.0;\n    const double zi = active ? pz[i] : 0.0;\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n    const int fullTiles = n / BS;\n    for (int tile = 0; k < BS; ++k) {\n            const double dx = sx[k] - xi;\n            const double dy = sy[k] - yi;\n            const double dz = sz[k] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = distanceSquared(dx, dy, dz);\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            Fx = fma(dx, invDist3, Fx);\n            Fy = fma(dy, invDist3, Fy);\n            Fz = fma(dz, invDist3, Fz);\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    // Remainder tile\n    const int tailStart = fullTiles * BS;\n    const int tail = n - tailStart;\n    if (tail > 0) {\n        if (threadIdx.x < tail) {\n            const int j = tailStart + threadIdx.x;\n            sx[threadIdx.x] = px[j];\n            sy[threadIdx.x] = py[j];\n            sz[threadIdx.x] = pz[j];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        for (int k = 0; Bodies are distributed\n// cyclically across ranks because the work per i shrinks with i.\n__global__ void potentialKernel(const double* __restrict__ px, const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                const double* __restrict__ pz, double* __restrict__ partial, const int n,\n                                const int rank, const int numRanks) {\n    const int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int i = rank + idx * numRanks;\n    if (i >= n) return;\n\n    const double xi = px[i], yi = py[i], zi = pz[i];\n    double sum = 0.0;\n    for (int j = i + 1; j < n; ++j) {\n        const double dx = px[j] - xi;\n        const double dy = py[j] - yi;\n        const double dz = pz[j] - zi;\n        const double dist = sqrt(distanceSquared(dx, dy, dz));\n (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - const std::vector<double>& potentialPartials) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)\n    for (const double partial : potentialPartials) {\n        energy += partial;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const       status[i] = 1;\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(nodeRank % deviceCount));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));  // force context creation outside the timed region\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = Positions are replicated on every rank (needed by every interaction),\n// velocities only exist for the bodies a rank owns.\nstruct DeviceState {\n    double *px = nullptr, *py = nullptr, *pz = nullptr;  // n entries (all bodies)\n    double *vx = nullptr, *vy = nullptr, *vz = nullptr;  // count entries (owned bodies)\n    double* partial = nullptr;                           // n entries, potential-energy partials\n};\n\n// The reference build lets the host compiler contract these expressions into FMAs; the device code\n// spells the same contractions out explicitly (and is compiled with -fmad=false) so that both\n// produce bit-identical results.\n__device__ __forceinline__ double distanceSquared(const double dx, const double dy, const double dz) {\n    return fma(dz, dz, fma(dx, dx, dy * dy)) + SOFTENING;\n}\n\n// Force accumulation for the locally owned bodies. The inner loop walks j in ascending order,\n// exactly like the sequential reference, so the summation order (and thus the result) is preserved.\n// The block size doubles as the shared-memory tile size; it is chosen at launch time so that even a\n// small number of local bodies is spread over all SMs (see chooseBlockSize).\ntemplate <int BS>\n__global__ void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ px, const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                    const double* __restrict__ pz, double* __restrict__ vx, double* __restrict__ vy,\n                                    double* __restrict__ vz, const int n, const int start, const int count) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BS], sy[BS], sz[BS];\n\n    const int local = blockIdx.x * BS + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = local < count;\n    const int i = start + local;\n\n    const double xi = active ? px[i] : 0.0;\n    const double yi = active ? py[i] : 0.0;\n    const double zi = active ? pz[i] : 0.0;\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n    const int fullTiles = n / BS;\n    for (int tile = 0; tile < fullTiles; ++tile) {\n        const int j = tile * BS + threadIdx.x;\n        sx[threadIdx.x] = px[j];\n        sy[threadIdx.x] = py[j];\n        sz[threadIdx.x] = pz[j];\n        __syncthreads();\n\n#pragma unroll 8\n        for (int k = 0; k < BS; ++k) {\n            const double dx = sx[k] - xi;\n            const double dy = sy[k] - yi;\n            const double dz = sz[k] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = distanceSquared(dx, dy, dz);\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            Fx = fma(dx, invDist3, Fx);\n            Fy = fma(dy, invDist3, Fy);\n            Fz = fma(dz, invDist3, Fz);\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    // Remainder tile\n    const int tailStart = fullTiles * BS;\n    const int tail = n - tailStart;\n    if (tail > 0) {\n        if (threadIdx.x < tail) {\n            const int j = tailStart + threadIdx.x;\n            sx[threadIdx.x] = px[j];\n            sy[threadIdx.x] = py[j];\n            sz[threadIdx.x] = pz[j];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        for (int k = 0; k < tail; ++k) {\n            const double dx = sx[k] - xi;\n            const double dy = sy[k] - yi;\n            const double dz = sz[k] - zi;\n            const double distSqr = distanceSquared(dx, dy, dz);\n            const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n            const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n            Fx = fma(dx, invDist3, Fx);\n            Fy = fma(dy, invDist3, Fy);\n            Fz = fma(dz, invDist3, Fz);\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (active) {\n        vx[local] += DT * Fx;\n        vy[local] += DT * Fy;\n        vz[local] += DT * Fz;\n    }\n}\n\n__global__ void integrateKernel(double* __restrict__ px, double* __restrict__ py, double* __restrict__ pz,\n                                const double* __restrict__ vx, const double* __restrict__ vy,\n                                const double* __restrict__ vz, const int start, const int count) {\n    const int local = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (local >= count) return;\n    const int i = start + local;\n    px[i] = fma(vx[local], DT, px[i]);\n    py[i] = fma(vy[local], DT, py[i]);\n    pz[i] = fma(vz[local], DT, pz[i]);\n}\n\n// Potential-energy partial sums: partial[i] = sum_{j>i} 1/dist(i,j). Bodies are distributed\n// cyclically across ranks because the work per i shrinks with i.\n__global__ void potentialKernel(const double* __restrict__ px, const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                const double* __restrict__ pz, double* __restrict__ partial, const int n,\n                                const int rank, const int numRanks) {\n    const int idx = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const int i = rank + idx * numRanks;\n    if (i >= n) return;\n\n    const double xi = px[i], yi = py[i], zi = pz[i];\n    double sum = 0.0;\n    for (int j = i + 1; j < sum -= 1.0 / dist;\n    }\n    partial[i] = sum;\n}\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - const std::vector<double>& potentialPartials) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)\n    for (const double partial : potentialPartials) {\n        energy += partial;\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const std::vector<Body>& bodies) {\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n    // 0 = ok, 1 = non-finite, 2 = position out of bounds, 3 = velocity out of bounds\n    std::vector<unsigned char> status(n,       status[i] = 1;\n            continue;\n        }\n\n        // Check for extreme values (bodies shouldn't fly off to infinity)\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (std::abs(body.pos.x) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.y) > maxPos || std::abs(body.pos.z) > maxPos) {\n            status[i] = 2;\n        } else if (std::abs(body.vel.x) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.y) > maxVel || std::abs(body.vel.z) > maxVel) {\n            status[i] = 3;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Report the first offending body, matching the sequential order of the original check\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        switch (status[i]) {\n            case 0: continue;\n            case 1: printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\"); reasonable bounds\\n\"); return false;\n        }\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n the GPU for this rank based on its position within the node.\nstatic void selectDevice(MPI_Comm comm, int rank) {\n    MPI_Comm nodeComm;\n    MPI_Comm_split_type(comm, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(nodeRank % deviceCount));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));  // force context creation outside the timed region\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &provided);\n\n    int rank = 0, numRanks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n    const bool isRoot = (rank == 0);\n\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i CUDA_CHECK(cudaMallocHost(&hz, n * sizeof(double)));\n\n    std::vector<double> vxHost(count), vyHost(count), vzHost(count);\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d.py, hy, n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d.pz, hz, n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n rank\n    if (count > 0) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(vxHost.data(), d.vx, count * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(vyHost.data(), d.vy, count * Bodies are distributed\n// cyclically across ranks because the work per i shrinks with i.\n__global__ void potentialKernel(const double* __restrict__ px, const double* __restrict__ py,\n                                const double* __restrict__ pz, double* __restrict__ partial, const int n,\n                                const int rank, const int numRanks) {\n    const int idx = blockIdx.x * CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(nodeRank % deviceCount));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));  // force context creation outside the timed region\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int provided = Each step every rank integrates only its own bodies, then positions are exchanged with three `MPI_Allgatherv`s through pinned staging buffers. Ranks are picked via `MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED` so it works across nodes.\n- **CUDA**: the O(N²) force kernel is the hot path — one thread per owned body, tiled through shared memory, plus an integration kernel and a kernel for the validation-only potential energy (cyclically distributed, since the triangular loop is unbalanced). Positions/velocities are held in SoA form on the device across the whole run; only positions cross PCIe.\n- **OpenMP**: parallelizes the host-side O(N) work — SoA packing/unpacking, result serialization, and `validateSimulation`.\n\n**`nbody/CMakeLists.txt`**: compiles `nbody.cpp` as CUDA, finds MPI and OpenMP, `CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native`, keeps `-O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra` for host code. I disassembled the reference build and reproduced gcc's FMA contraction pattern explicitly in device code (compiled with `-fmad=false`), so results are **bit-identical to the original**: the `-r` hash matches for n = 1, 2, 7, 255, 257, 1000, 1024, 2048, 4099, 20000 at 1–4 ranks, and is invariant in the rank count.\n\nOne deliberate deviation: the potential energy is summed as per-body partials rather than one sequential chain, so `Final energy` can differ in its last printed digit (e.g. `113468155.086079` vs `...81`, ~1e-14 relative). Bodies' positions/velocities — the hashed output — are unaffected. Making this bit-exact would require the fully serial O(N²) accumulation.\n\n## Performance (4× RTX 3090, per-step FP64-throughput bound)\n\n| case | original (1 CPU core) | 1 GPU | 2 GPUs | 4 GPUs |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| n=8192, s=10 | 4952 ms | 86 ms | 57 ms | 55 ms |\n| n=40000, s=20 | ~2 min (est.) | 2956 ms | 1508 ms | 787 ms |\n\n~90× at n=8192 and ~3.8× multi-GPU scaling at n=40000 (small n has too few bodies per rank to fill four GPUs). The kernel runs at ~1.1e10 interactions/s per GPU, at the FP64 roofline given IEEE `sqrt`/division are required for bit-exactness.
